- **P21 交易计划板 + rotation 双重计分 bug 修复 (2026-07-02)**: owner 目标"系统帮我分析股票/市场并给出买什么/什么价/什么时候"。证据先行(3 路并行审计:模型解剖/量化证据/推荐面盘点)确认:原子组件全部已存在且有测试(rerank 排序、7 档 ladder、chase-risk、tradability、Rule 12 纪律、sizing 能力),缺的只是**汇装器 + 展示面**;并当场发现 **live bug(P21-01)**——`hybrid_rerank._rotation_adjustment` 对 `annotate_rotation` 已折入 `rotation_score` 的调整二次施加,live 管线 extended 龙头实际 −0.30 / 二线 +0.16 = Rule 11.14.5 文档值(−0.15/+0.08)的**两倍**;已用 reason-marker 幂等修复 + TDD 回归锁死。落地:**Rule 11.16(交易计划板诚实)**——计划≠预测、只继承排序不新增预测分、planStatus fail-closed(study_only/leader_extended 强制 watch_only)、Rule 11.6 价位名词、Rule 11.8 算术仓位(1% NAV 风险预算 + 20% 集中度帽 + 整手/S株 双算,无 NAV 不虚构)、事实性时机清单、常驻无 edge 披露;新 `reporting/action_board.py` + `/api/dashboard` 挂 `actionBoard`(fail-open→null)+ 共享 `ActionBoardCard` 挂 V1-V4(Rule 11.7 parity)。**验证**:focused 97 / full smoke **1586 passed, 5 deselected, 0 failed**(基线 1570→1586,+16 全新,零回归)。**诚实边界不变:板只重组已有结构参考(建议价位/止损参考/目标参考/仓位算术/条件清单),不宣称任何预测能力;无概率/胜率/期望收益。** 未 commit。详见 Change Log 2026-07-02 P21 行 + `docs/01_TASKS.md` P21。
- **Edge 搜索诚实收口 + 系统定位明确 (2026-07-02)**: owner 追问"系统到底能做什么/是不是废物"。用 **3+ 年数据**把能测的信号全测了——**price_reversal(798 天,DSR 0.87)/ disclosure-drift(1 年 251 天/21,682 披露,DSR 0.50,负 IC)/ 因子动物园(low-vol/动量/high52w,best DSR 0.58)全不过反过拟合门;value/quality 因本库财报覆盖太稀疏(仅 2–3 有效横截面)测不了**。**结论(证据这次最全):在能测数据上无 demonstrated 可交易 edge —— 不是"再攒数据"能解决,是信号本身没 edge。** 系统**不是**信号/印钞机(现在诚实地知道大概率做不到),**是**能用的作战台:实时数据核查错误前提、拦负 EV/FOMO 单(本会话实拦一笔周末隔夜单,周一低开 5–7%)、可交易性/成本/S株/集中度、Event Desk priced-in、诚实记账、拒造假胜率。**诚实最优 = 被动 1306.T 打底 + 系统当决策支持/纪律工具**。本会话新增基建(保留,通用):`tools/forward_signal_report.py`+`daily_routine` 非致命 wiring(§16 live forward harness)、TDnet poll 接进 `daily_routine`(语料每日累积,forward-only)、3 个历史回测工具(price_reversal/disclosure-drift/factor-zoo)、双周 DSR OS 计划任务 `HTR_DSR_Progress`。**唯一未被否的方向**:采日本财报历史数据测 value/quality(owner 定夺,是采数据不是等)。dashboard 服务已重启加载至今数据(PID 变动)。数据采集 06-29/06-30 afterclose 均 ok。未 commit。详见 Change Log 2026-07-01/02。
- **P20-RC release-candidate prep + adversarial review (2026-06-26) — 裁决:不是干净自洽的 P20 RC**: 按发布候选 6 任务推进。**Scope=Option B(后端+前端可见)**——API 已暴露 SKHY 字段,故 `frontend/src/htr-shared.jsx` 共享 `CatalystBadges` 补只读 SKHY chip(`skhyCatalystActive` gate、off/watch/stale/pending/unavailable 不渲染、纯研究措辞"非买入建议,不预测收益"、**不重排候选**)+2 前端契约测试。`git add` 恰好 **9 个必需新文件**(不夹带无关脏文件、不回退他人改动)。**验证**(`.runtime`):focused **81** / semi **52** / full smoke `-m "not slow"` **1570 passed, 5 deselected, 0 failed** / slow **5 passed** / 前端契约 **45 passed** / `git diff --check`(staged)clean。**3-agent 对抗复审**:前端治理 **PASS** + Rule 11.15 端到端 **PASS**(SKHY 仅外部 lane、decision_log/calibration 零引用、fail-open、shadow)+ **packaging FAIL**。**发布裁决=NOT a clean self-contained P20 RC**:① staged `skhy_event_review.py:77` import 未跟踪 `forward_signal_eval` → 仅发 9 个会 ImportError;② P20 功能改动散在 9 个之外且 untracked/未 staged——前端 badge `htr-shared.jsx`(untracked)、`tools/daily_routine.py`(untracked)、`api/serializers.py`(脏但**未 staged**→ 9 个连 SKHY 字段都不吐);③ 一堆传递依赖(`s_kabu_universe`/`build_s_kabu_overlay`/`catalyst`/`hybrid_rerank`/`theme_rotation`/`theme_leaders`/`ticker_metadata`/`stock_news_fetcher`)全 untracked。**无 calibrated edge/概率/胜率主张;overlay 仍 shadow。** 真 RC 需 owner 拍板项目级落库(跟踪整条 P20 依赖闭包,≈首次正式提交)。**未 commit/tag/release(未授权)**。详见 Change Log 2026-06-26 P20-RC 行 + `docs/01_TASKS.md` P20-RC。

- **P20 SKHY ADR + Japan-Semi overlay IMPLEMENTED (2026-06-25)**: 计划全部落地执行(TDD,先红后绿)。新增 `data/external/adr_watch.py`(纯 schema,状态枚举/is_stale/无 edge 字段)、`tools/refresh_skhy_adr_watch.py`(注入 fetcher;SKHY 无报价=`pending_listing` 绝不替换它符号;non-fatal 接入 `daily_routine` after macro/before screener)、`candidate_engine/skhy_overlay.py`(off/watch/active 事件态+半衰期 freshness;000660.KS 同公司不算独立确认;JP 半导体 sympathy 须 ≥2 facts、板块成员单独不够、Rule 11.14 extended-chase 不加分、capped[-0.05,+0.07])、`reporting/skhy_event_review.py`(live-only event clusters、同日去重、不混 backdated、默认 `insufficient_data`、本 harness 永不授予 promotion);`api/serializers` 暴露 `meta.dataQuality.adrWatch` + `eventDesk.skhy`(**fail-open**:无快照=unavailable / JP 排序不变 / "no probability" 免责无值)。**P20-06 手动 ADR journal = SKIPPED**(可选,未被要求)。**验证**:focused **72** / semi-regression **48** / full smoke `-m "not slow"` **1559 passed, 5 deselected(slow vectorbt), 0 failed**(`.runtime` basetemp)。SKHY = 外部催化输入,非 JP 候选 / 非校准 edge;无 probability/win-rate/expected-return/edge 语言;overlay 维持 **shadow**(晋级须 live-only 前向证据过 ADR-0010 + Rule 16)。逐任务细节见本文件 Change Log(2026-06-25 P20 行)+ `docs/01_TASKS.md` P20-01~08。advice-only 未绕过;未 commit。**已知坑(非阻塞)**:JP watchlist 校验 `\d{4}.T` 拒新式字母数字东证代码(Kioxia `285A.T`)——adr_watch lane 自身接受,若将来要把 285A 放进 JP watchlist 需放宽校验。

- **P18-06 dashboard candidate-name hot update + 7740.T classification (2026-06-22)**: 用户问为什么系统推荐 `7740.T`，并要求面板不只显示股票代号也显示名字。诊断：`7740.T` 排第一不是新闻催化/主题龙头，而是 `screener_v2` 动量+流动性候选；截至 2026-06-19 snapshot，`mom_20=+33.13%`、`mom_60=+30.75%`、`sharpe_20=0.40`、`adv_rank=0.839`、`fundamental_score=1.0`、`qualityPenalty=0`，所以在 sector-only/追高候选被新规则惩罚后排到 top；其 `newsCatalyzed=false`、`isThemeLeader=false`，不应解读为强推荐。热更新实现：`api/serializers.py` 从 `ticker_metadata` 读取 metadata `name` 填入 candidate `nameJa`，前端 V1/V2/V3/V4 原本已读 `nameJa/nameCn`，无需 UI 大改；新增回归测试 `test_real_candidates_include_metadata_company_name`。Live 验证：重启服务 PID **56764**，`/api/dashboard?top_n=3` 返回 `7740.T · Tamron Co.,Ltd.`、`2865.T · Global X Nasdaq 100 Covered Call ETF`、`7600.T · Japan Medical Dynamic Marketing, INC.`。分类结论：Tamron 官方业务是 Photographic Products / Surveillance & FA Lenses / Mobility & Healthcare，FA/Machine Vision lenses 可算 `optical` / `machine_vision` adjacent，但不是典型 semi 设备股；系统不应硬挂 `semi`，只有出现半导体检测/wafer inspection 等公司级证据时才临时关联。验证：focused **26 passed** (`test_api_dashboard`, `test_ticker_metadata`)，full unit **1449 passed / 1 pytest-cache permission warning**。未 commit。

- **P18-03 forward walk-forward join hardening + calibration gate check (2026-06-18)**: 用户想解锁 calibration。按 Rule 8.2.3/9.4 先走真实 forward validator,不强行覆盖。复跑 `tools/validate_calibration_walk_forward.py --origin live --asof 2026-06-18`: zero-join 症状已不复现,当前 **n_joined_samples=529 / n_joined_date_clusters=11**,foldable OOS **n_samples=300 / n_effective_date_clusters=6**。已加诊断字段 `n_joined_samples`/`n_joined_date_clusters` 到 `purged_walk_forward` report,并用 TDD 加回归测试 `test_report_exposes_joined_sample_counts_before_folding` 防未来静默 0 join。验证:targeted **10 passed**; live validator verdict **insufficient_data**; daily smoke with `.runtime` basetemp **1450 passed / 5 deselected** (`.pytest_tmp` 在本 shell 权限锁导致 308 setup errors,改用 `.runtime` 后全绿)。**不能解锁**:有效 OOS clusters 6 < locked floor 20,且 model Brier **0.2846** 差于 climatology/stratified **0.2407**,CI [-0.0666,-0.0228] 为负。允许的 "pre-unlock" 只有 shadow/diagnostic calibration(显式标诊断,不升 `score_status`,不显示概率/胜率,不进 advice);任何 `calibrated_probability` UI promotion 必须等 >=20 live clusters + beat random/climatology/stratified + CI>0 + leakage clean。未 commit。
- **Review fix pass (2026-06-15)**: 修复全面审查指出的 3 个高价值缺口。① `purged_walk_forward` 现在按 Rule 8.2.3 强制锁定 ship 条件：≥20 independent date clusters、打赢 random/climatology/stratified 全部基准、date-cluster CI 过零、leakage verdict 必须 clean；当前 live validation 仅 7 clusters → `insufficient_data`，不会误 pass。② daily smoke `SMOKE_CMD` 固定 `--basetemp .pytest_tmp/daily-smoke`，规避 Windows user Temp 权限/锁导致的 scheduled preopen 假红。③ dashboard `meta.dataQuality.newsCatalyst` 读取最新 afterclose 记录，显式暴露 `news_refresh_rc` / `meta_refresh_rc` degradation，避免新闻/元数据失败时仍让催化 badge 看起来无条件可信。TDD 红→绿；验证：targeted 11 passed；related 42 passed；full smoke **1399 passed / 5 deselected**；slow lane **5 passed / 1399 deselected**。仍有 requests dependency warning（urllib3/chardet 与 requests 支持范围不匹配）需后续锁依赖。未 commit。

## Current Objective

> **2026-06-25 当前快照(P19 横截面研究 + S株 执行 + 数据修复)** — 详细逐条见 Change Log 06-23~06-25
>
> 本阶段从"建系统 / cutover"转入"**找独立 edge + 用系统**"。
> - **P19 横截面 + 信号叠加(ADR-0011 / §16)**:建了成本 break-even 闸门 `backtesting/forward_signal_eval` + `signal_library`(一键过 §16 闸门)。否决了原 screener `buy` 分数(live 横截面 Rank-IC 为负)。**找到项目首个独立候选 `price_reversal`(5–10D 价格反转,5D Rank-IC +0.13 / t≈3.2,与 buy 正交 ρ−0.2)——但 Deflated Sharpe 0.64 未过晋级门,仅 15 天/单一 regime → 已正确归档:不交易,等前向累积 ~2–3 月后重 gate**(P19-03 standing)。
> - **S株 执行模式端到端接入(Rule 5.2)**:tradability S株 分支 → `s_kabu_universe` overlay(held+watchlist,ADR-0005 安全)→ producer CLI → daily_routine 每日产出 → `/api/dashboard.sKabuOverlay` → 前端独立卡片。贵价票(8035.T 等)首次进系统视野。
> - **价格数据修复(#8)**:回填 TEL/Advantest 6 月缺口 + Kioxia 全历史;`refresh_htr_price_db` 现把 held+watchlist 纳入每日续更(非 universe 持仓不再断档)。
> - **重心**:① daily routine 持续累积前向样本,到量后重 gate `price_reversal`;② 用系统(dashboard `:8000` 在线)。**被动核心 1306.T 仍是诚实最优;无已验证 edge。**
> - **已知限制(非阻塞)**:(a) Kioxia `285A.T` 等**新式字母数字东证代码**被 watchlist 校验 `\d{4}.T` 拒(待定:放宽);(b) S株 卡片目前是独立浮层,未并入主候选面板统一卡。
>
> ——以下为历史(W1-W7 建设期 / cutover 期),保留备查——

Phase 1 W1-W7 主体已完成。当前重心从"建系统"转到"cutover 执行 + 用系统 + W8+ 增强"。

**已落地 (W1-W7)**:

1. **数据新鲜度层** (W1, P10-14/16/19)：TDnet RSS + 多源延迟价格 orchestrator + J-Quants 接口 (deferred 待凭据)。Cycle 2 网络层余 briefing integration pending。
2. **Time-to-first-value 可用层** (W2, P10-20/17)：Daily Advisory Cockpit Stage 0 + Silent Watchlist Intelligence (event detector + monitor + silent queue)。
3. **HTR 自有持仓** (W2, P10-21/22/23 + ADR-0008 + Section 14)：append-only journal + Rule 14.2 fail-closed validation + atomic transitions + migration script with NAV diff verify + manual entry CLI/API/service。**Cutover T = 2026-05-27 Wednesday JST, real run scheduled ≥15:00 JST close** (用户 2026-05-27 08:43 JST 拍板)。Rule 14.8 canonical dry-run 将在 ≥15:00 JST 收盘后紧贴真跑前执行并留证。
4. **Push 模式骨架** (W3, P10-18/10)：Rule 12.1-12.6 全部 (alert budget / stale fail-closed / chase filter / cooling-off / concentration / cross-strategy journal) + 三个 guarded notifier channels (desktop/email/telegram) 默认 disabled + GuardedAlert type-enforced。
5. **反思系统全 8 layers** (W4-W6, P11-00..07 + ADR-0007)：PIT Ledger / Trace Logger / Event Detector (CUSUM+ARL bootstrap+Holm) / Policy Replay (NOT Pearl + Data Freshness Gate) / RCA (NOT Shapley + marginal_recovery) / LLM Reflection (Rule 8.3.1+13.4 双重 regex + Rule 13.12 action boundary) / Human Decision Gate (Rule 13.5-13.17) / Meta-Reflection (Rule 13.10/13.16)。
6. **Calibration bootstrap** (W4, P10-13 + ADR-0006)：历史回填工具 + evidence_origin 字段 + scanner_config_hash 校验。

**W8+ 增强 (剩余非阻塞)**:

- P10-14 Cycle 2 Windows Task Scheduler 注册脚本真实执行（需 admin shell，用户自跑）

## Active Decision

1. **Project_optimized + Project_v5 只读消费** (ADR-0005)：HTR 不写回 sibling project；任何外部数据源（TDnet / Yahoo JP / Kabutan / J-Quants）落 HTR-native `reports/{tdnet,observability,traces,reflections}/`。
2. **选项 C — Backdated Calibration Bootstrap** (ADR-0006, Rule 8.2.1)：选 1-2 天历史回填解锁第一个真胜率，而非等 4-8 周 forward 样本积累；严格 sunset 机制 — forward 样本达 ≥100 自动切回 live-only。
3. **Codex 4-role 评审采纳** (ADR-0007)：反思系统 Layer 3 是 Policy Replay 不是 Pearl do-calculus；Layer 4 是 Root Cause Analysis (ablation + funnel + diagnostics) 不是 Shapley；L0 PIT Observability Ledger 加为 foundation；CUSUM 阈值从 ARL bootstrap 派生不用 generic σ 倍数；语言 conditional ("under reconstructed U and C")。
4. **LLM 边界 — 叙事综合 only** (Rule 8.3.1 + 13.4)：gemma4:e4b 默认；post-generation regex 拦截任何 `\d+%` / 胜率 / 概率 / probability / likelihood；24h 缓存防 GPU/VRAM 冲突。
5. **No TwelveData / No J-Quants accounts is permanent** (2026-05-25 user confirmed)：active price chain 永久从 6 源剪枝到 **`cache → yahoo_japan → kabutan → yfinance`** 3 源。Stooq 服务政策变更同日剪掉。inactive 代码保留为未来 optionality（19 J-Quants 测试 + 完整 auth flow）。
6. **Anti-FOMO 治理优先于 calibration purity** (Phase 1 v2 重排)：fresh data + push + 反追涨纪律 → 然后才做 backdated calibration。Codex 验证 "calibration 给样本但样本不防 FOMO"。
7. **Time-to-first-value > academic prestige**：Pearl/Shapley 标签放弃；时间表诚实从 6-7 周改为 10-12 周（Codex realistic）。
8. **Activation staging** (Rule 12.0, 2026-05-26)：为了尽快可用，允许 Stage 0 Pull-only cockpit 与 Stage 1 silent alerts 先上线；真实 desktop/email/telegram push 必须等 Stage 2 guarded push，即 P10-18 discipline filter 通过后才启用。
9. **HTR Single Source of Truth Migration** (ADR-0008 Proposed, Section 14 binding-on-T, 2026-05-26)：HTR 自建 append-only portfolio journal 取代 `position_adapter.py` 只读 Project_optimized；cutover day T 待用户确认（当前候选：2026-05-30 周六 / 2026-06-07 周日）。T 后 Project_optimized portfolios 冻结为历史归档，HTR 是 live positions / cash / realized P&L 唯一真相源。Rule 3 / Rule 8.2 / Rule 9.4 / §10 gate 8 全保留——manual fill 是用户报告已通过外部券商成交，不是 HTR 下单。Rule 14.6：calibration sample 排除 `paper/migration/correction` source 防双计数。Rule 14.8：T 必须写绝对日期 + 已校验星期；真跑前必须重新 dry-run 并留证。

**Local Beta v0 gate for Monday 2026-06-01 (P10-27, highest priority before new features)**:

1. **Freeze scope**: no new user-facing trading features before Monday. Only readiness, smoke, runbook, slow-test segregation, and rollback cleanup.
2. **Rollback point**: review dirty working tree, isolate unrelated sibling-project changes, and create a rollback point (commit, tagged stash, or documented backup). Do not rely on "remember what changed".
3. **Daily smoke lane**: use a fast pre-open verification lane covering API/dashboard/profile/calibration/watchlist/proposals/manual-recording/frontend contracts. Current evidence from 2026-05-30: beta smoke subset `182 passed`; unit lane excluding vectorbt `1245 passed`; integration lane excluding vectorbt backtest `23 passed`.
4. **Slow research lane**: vectorbt / numba tests are separated. Current root cause evidence: pytest stalls in `vectorbt -> numba.core.caching.ensure_cache_path -> tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile`, so full `pytest tests` is not the Monday readiness command until this is isolated or marked slow.
5. **Localhost launch check**: start the dashboard locally and verify no page errors, real candidates load, freshness/session labels are visible, and calibration remains downgraded / insufficient unless a valid Rule 9.4.1 ship verdict exists.
6. **Operating rhythm**: pre-open smoke, pull-only dashboard during session, manual record only after an external broker action, after-close `emit_daily_predictions.py` then `sweep_pending_outcomes.py` to accumulate forward samples.
7. **Stop conditions**: if mock/degraded data is hidden, calibration downgrade disappears incorrectly, NAV/holdings derivation is incoherent, or any broker/order/paper/live-signal route appears outside the manual-recording carve-out, Monday mode becomes debugging-only.

- 三轮独立 review: 11 HIGH/BLOCKER + 5 MEDIUM 全 patched
- **W8 cutover (2026-05-27 15:55 JST)**: ADR-0008 cutover；Project_optimized 端补跑 5-26 account_snapshot 解决 ¥300 stale-snapshot NAV 漂移；HTR journal + marker 落地；HTR-as-SSoT 守卫双向锁死 (read via journal-first + write via `_refuse_htr_ssot_write` guard)
- **W8 calibration 链 (2026-05-27 ~ 2026-05-28)**: P10-13 bootstrap CLI + 3 周 762 paired samples (2026-03-23..04-13)；isotonic recalibration v1 Brier 0.3422 → 0.2427；wire 到 /profile + V3；forward live pipeline (emit/sweep + sunset infra)；50 live preds emitted 2026-05-27
- **W8 UX (2026-05-28)**: P10-06 LLM Per-Ticker Brief module + /llm_brief endpoint + V3 折叠面板；K-line crosshair (broker-app OHLCV tooltip) + V3 K 线面板让 crosshair 可见；scanner refresh selected_tickers 30 天 stale → today
- **TradingAgents 调研 (2026-05-28)**: 80.1k stars / v0.2.5 / 仍 US-only / 无 calibration/PIT/regex 守门；**不换平台**；borrow targets: Bull/Bear/Judge (⭐⭐⭐⭐) + analyst roles (⭐⭐⭐) + LlmProvider 抽象 (⭐⭐)

**红线 (从未放宽)**: Rule 3 advice-only / Rule 8.2 PIT mandatory / Rule 8.3 LLM no probability / Rule 9.4 win rate threshold / §10 gate 8 broker hard-block / **§14 HTR-as-SSoT 自 2026-05-27 起强制** / **Rule 8.2.1 sunset 自动化 ready 2026-05-28**。

**关键测量 (in-sample brier，3D horizon，evidence_origin=bootstrap)**：
- raw screener score brier 0.3422 (比随机 0.25 差 — 数学证据：score 不是 probability)
- isotonic calibrated brier 0.2427 (**in-sample 低于随机但 K-fold OOS 揭穿过拟合**)
- 系统性过自信 ~2x：score 0.84 bin (n=558) 实际 actual_freq=0.50
- Top-bucket 信号：score 0.91+ bin (n=119) 实际 actual_freq=0.66 — top ranking 有 +15pp 概率优势

**K-fold OOS 验证 (block-temporal 5-fold, 2026-05-28)**：
- OOS calibrated Brier mean **0.2823 ± 0.0660** (vs in-sample 0.2427，差距 ~16%)
- fold 0-2 (早期 3-23..04-03) OOS 0.24 接近 in-sample
- fold 3 (04-08..09) OOS 0.2654 边缘
- **fold 4 (04-10..13) OOS 0.4132 灾难** — 比 raw_brier 0.4103 还差，calibration 完全没泛化能力
- folds_below_random = 3/5
- 数学证据：**in-sample 0.2427 是严重 overfit，没有真实 OOS 信号**
- Rule 9.4 verdict = **downgrade**，V3 LeaderCard 自动退回 uncalibrated_research_score

Phase 1 时间表：原 10-12 周计划压缩到 ~30 小时 (5-27 ~ 5-28) 完成 W1-W7 主体 + W8 cutover + calibration 链全闭环 + K 线 UX。剩余 = forward sample 累积等 sunset 触发 + W8+ P10 P1/P2 增强（全部非阻塞）+ TradingAgents borrow（Bull/Bear/Judge 等）。

Status branches: `future_cutoff` (eval date < cutoff), `symbol_not_found` (fetcher empty or raised), `insufficient_data` (some/no horizons evaluable), `complete` (≥ max horizon trading days). Ladder touch detection: below-tier (aggressive/balanced/conservative entry + stop) hit when bar.low ≤ tier price; above-tier (first/second/stretch exit) hit when bar.high ≥ tier price. Reference price falls back to first bar's open if `prediction.extra["reference_price"]` is missing. Re-evaluating same prediction on later date yields new outcome_id (so insufficient_data can be upgraded to complete by re-running). Reason: §10 gate 4 is the blocker between §8.6 decision logging (P9-01 done) and §9.4 calibration (P9-03 pending). Without outcome join, the predictions logged in `reports/predictions/` cannot produce any feedback signal. P9-03 calibration engine now has its input schema and storage convention. | 2026-05-24 | P9-03 done — Calibration Engine complete via 2 cycles. §10 gate 5 in place. New module `src/hot_theme_rotator/calibration/` with `CalibrationReport` schema (source ∈ {opportunity, attribution}, horizon_days, sample_count, status ∈ {calibrated, insufficient_calibration}, brier_score, log_loss, bins), `CalibrationBin` (empty bins carry nan to be unambiguous), and math primitives (Brier, clamped log loss to avoid log(0), 10 equal-width reliability bins with last-bin right-closed). Reporter `build_calibration_report(predictions, outcomes, source, horizon_days, min_samples=100)` pairs by `prediction_id`, skips outcomes whose `status != 'complete'`, derives bullish-only ground truth from `realized_returns[f"{horizon}D"] > 0`. Schema enforces §9.4: `status='calibrated'` requires `sample_count >= min_samples_required` AND brier+log_loss non-null; `status='insufficient_calibration'` forbids any numeric metrics or bins (so a partial sample can never accidentally publish a "win rate"). Default `min_samples = 100`; configurable per caller. Reason: §10 gate 5 was the last math layer needed before the system can ever escape the `uncalibrated_research_score` label (per §9.4 + §10). With predictions persistence (P9-01) + outcome join (P9-02) + calibration math now all present, the only remaining piece for actually computing a calibrated probability is real outcome data accumulating in `reports/outcomes/` — that needs `LegacyDailyPriceFetcher` (P9-02 follow-up) and 100+ paired samples. Math layer is fully validated against known-answer fixtures; brier(0.5, all-1s) == 0.25, log_loss clamps to -log(eps), 10 bins reach correct cells. No score_status values renamed; no `calibrated_probability` ever published by UI yet (would need real samples). ## Open Risks

**数据 / 服务层 (近期暴露)**

- **Stooq 服务政策 2026-05-25 变更** — 免费 CSV 下载现需 captcha 申请 apikey；P10-19 active chain 已剪除，但显示外部服务条款可单方面变更，是 scraping fallback 的结构性风险。
- **active price chain 仅 3 源** (yahoo_japan / kabutan / yfinance)，且前两个是 HTML scrape。HTML 结构改动 → parser 立即坏。Cycle 2 必须加 daily health check (Codex 推荐 — `health_check()` 每日跑 + endpoint status + parse 成功 + freshness 记录)。
- **Yahoo JP + Kabutan consensus 不是真独立验证** (Codex 警告)：两源很可能反映同一上游 JPX 数据，consensus check 主要是 parser failure detector，不是 feed truth verification。Rule 12.3 chase filter 决策时这个限制必须传达给用户。
- **TwelveData + J-Quants 用户无账号** (永久约束 2026-05-25)：高 salience 时无真正"独立 API 仲裁者"。inactive 代码保留为未来 optionality。
- **反思系统数据源 stale 风险** (Codex 警告)：无 J-Quants → Policy Replay 只能用 `Project_optimized/japan_market.db.daily_prices` (已 4-6 周 stale)。P11-03 acceptance 加 "data freshness gate" 拒绝 stale > N trading days 运行。
- **`Project_optimized/selected_tickers.json` asof = 2026-04-27 / `factor_signals` asof = 2026-04-13**：dashboard 与 morning_briefing `--source db` 实质是 4-6 周前数据。必须用 `--source yfinance` 或刷新 Project_optimized 才能用候选做今日判断。

**策略 / 校准层**

- **策略高胜率验证缺口**：需用盈亏比、尾部亏损、最大回撤、换仓成本验证，不能只看命中率。
- **A股 / 美股跨市场温度**：不能直接等价为可交易信号，仍作为风控 + 主题背景。
- **Backdated calibration 历史回填**：ADR-0006 + Rule 8.2.1 已加严格守护，但操作员必须用连续历史窗口（非 cherry-pick）+ git-pinned scanner config hash + 写 bootstrap_provenance.json — 这些自动化由 P10-13 实施。

**实施 / 工程层 (Codex 评审 2026-05-25 提出)**

- **Codex 评审 "not yet decision-grade for high-salience intraday alerts"**：W1 实施过了 mock-HTTP 测试 + live smoke 部分 endpoint 通过，但还没在真实生产环境下连续跑过一天。任何 Rule 12.3 chase 决策现在仍需用户人工最终确认。
- **YanoshinTdnetAdapter rate limit 并发 bug**：per-instance state 不防多个 Task Scheduler 实例并发。Cycle 2 解决方案：Windows Task `/Z` flag 禁止重叠运行（用户态 mitigation），文件锁留作 Cycle 2 之后。
- **`JquantsLiveBridge` idToken TTL 缺失**：缓存 idToken 但无 TTL 跟踪；24h 后过期会 401，无自动 refresh。但用户无账号 → 影响为零；激活前必须解决。
- **PriceQuote `data_ts_inferred=True` 时 Rule 12.2 stale 检查需要额外严格**：HTML scrape 默认 inferred，意味着我们其实不知道 quote 真实新鲜度；调用方在 chase filter / stop / exit 决策时必须额外保守。
- **旧项目编码乱码**：迁移文档时以代码行为和测试为准，不以可视编码为准。
## Rules Snapshot

**单一来源原则**

- 单一状态文件：只更新 `PROJECT_STATUS.md`。
- 单一任务入口：所有工作必须挂到 `docs/01_TASKS.md`。
- 单一治理入口：所有修改规则以 `docs/02_GOVERNANCE.md` 为准。
- 单一目录职责：`docs/03_FOLDER_MAP.md` 明确每个子包 / `reports/` 子目录归属。

**绝对红线 (Rule 3 / 8.2 / 8.3 / 9.4 / §10 / §12 / §13)**

- 单一执行约束：未通过 paper gate 前不允许自动下单 (Rule 3 + §10 gate 8 hard-forbidden)。
- PIT mandatory: every input has `available_ts`; `available_ts > decision_cutoff` 必须 fail-closed (Rule 8.2)。Rule 8.2.1 backdated 例外仅限 `tools/backdated_calibration_bootstrap.py` 一个工具。
- LLM 不出概率: post-generation regex 拦截 `\d+%` / 胜率 / 概率 / probability / likelihood (Rule 8.3 + 8.3.1 + 13.4)。
- Calibrated win rate label 需 `sample_count >= 100` + brier + log_loss (Rule 9.4)。Bootstrap evidence 必须过同一关。
- Push discipline 全部 6 子规则 (Section 12.1-12.6): alert budget / stale fail-closed / chase filter / cooling-off / concentration guard / cross-strategy journal — P10-18 实施前 push 通道不开启。
- Reflection discipline 全部 10 子规则 (Section 13.1-13.10): proposals only / counterfactual real OHLC / sample size tiered / LLM 不出概率 / 7d expiry / metadata required / pre-post backtest / no single-event / log rejects / meta-reflection。

**过程规则 (post Codex 评审 2026-05-25 新增)**

- §2.1 External Adapter Fixture Provenance: 新 adapter 的 fixture 必须从真实 capture 而非凭印象造；保存到 `tests/fixtures/captured/{source}/{date}_{desc}.{ext}`；live smoke pass 才能标 task done。
- 学术标签自检: 在用 Pearl do-calculus / Shapley value / 等 strong-claim 术语前，必须先验证语义前提（因果图 / cooperative game well-defined）；不满足 → 用 honest 替代 (policy replay / RCA)。
